# The O/P/Q/U-family inventory (plan §3.4; R1)

Generated by `python dev/store_rename_slices/pqu_scan.py` from the tree at `v2026.4.0` (`t3toolbox/`, `tests/`, `examples/` equal the tag). The classification table is `CLASSIFY` in that script (ordered, reviewed entry by entry; every unmapped candidate must match one or the run fails). Disposition per §3.4: frame core `P`/`Q` -> `L`/`R` (LR); a frame's down store -> `D`, its up store (`O`/`U`) -> `U` (STORE); a variation/direction of a frame down core -> `D` forms (DFORM); a T3's own Tucker core -> `B` (B) and its direction, gradient or Hessian block -> `B` forms (BFORM); `KEEP:<§1.5 item>`; `ASK` (not settled by the plan). MAPPED = the dry run (`dev/store_rename_apply.py`, tiers A-C) already renames the occurrence.

**Slices.** `R2`: applied in R2 from `dev/store_rename_letter_sites.md` (inventory-backed, plan-enumerated, a frame core `P`/`Q`, or completing a tier-C binding). `R2-proposed`: a `B`/`D` row (or its simultaneous `O -> U` partner) beyond tier C's lists — on the letter-site list but NOT loaded until the reviewer agrees (§3.4 "R1 gate"). `R4a/R4b/R5 hand`: text. `kept`: listed in `pqu_keeps.tsv` / `pqu_text_keeps.tsv` for `dnt.txt` and K8's keep list.
